A categorical equivalence for odd or even involutive FL e -chains
Sándor Jenei

Abstract
. We lift the representation of the class ℑ 01 𝔠 \mathfrak{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}} of odd or even involutive FL e -chains by the class 𝔅 𝔊 \mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{G}} of bunches of layer groups (a class of direct systems of abelian o o -groups) published in [ 10 ] , to a categorical equivalence between the category ℐ 01 𝔠 \mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}} of odd or even involutive FL e -chains equipped with their normal homomorphisms and the category ℬ 𝒢 \mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}} of bunches of layer groups equipped with bunch homomorphisms, defined here. Restricting this equivalence only to odd or only to even involutive FL e -chains or to further subclasses thereof (e.g. to Sugihara chains) yields further categorical equivalences.



2010 Mathematics Subject Classification
Primary 97H50, 20M30; Secondary 06F05, 06F20, 03B47.

1.
Introduction

The notion of categorical equivalence plays a fundamental role in category theory.
Proving categorical equivalences is relevant, besides category theory, in several other fields of mathematics, too.
If two categories are equivalent then “essentially the same theorems hold” in the two categories;
an equivalence of categories preserves all “categorical” concepts and properties.
The functor which shows the equivalence of the categories
creates the opportunity to ”translate” theorems between different kinds of mathematical structures, such that the essential meaning of those theorems is preserved under the translation.
A categorical equivalence is often used as a “bridge theorem”: if the objects and morphisms are better understood in one of the categories then problems in the other category can be settled by solving the “translated” version of the problem.
We present a few examples in the realm of non-classical logics, taking into consideration that topology, algebra, categories, and logic are intertwined areas.
In
[
6
]
, N. Galatos and J. G. Raftery established a category equivalence between (semilinear) generalized Sugihara monoids and nuclear relative Stone algebras. This result has been generalized by W. Chen in
[
14
]
to a category equivalence between semiconic generalized Sugihara monoids and strong nuclear Brouwerian algebras.
A whole lot of fundamental categorical equivalences under the name Stone equivalence are between topological spaces and partially ordered sets.
A well-known categorical equivalence is the
\Gamma
functor by D. Mundici between the category of MV-algebras and the category of abelian
\ell
-groups with strong unit
[
13
]
. This result has a precursor by C. C. Chang in
[
3
]
and
has been generalized by A. Dvurečenskij in
[
4
]
and by
N. Galatos and C. Tsinakis in
[
7
]
.
Several categorical equivalences concerning subcategories of those odd involutive residuated lattices where the unit element has a Boolean complement
have been established in
[
2
]
.
In the present paper we prove a categorical equivalence between all odd or even involutive (pointed) residuated chains and a category of direct systems of abelian
o
-groups.
More precisely, we lift the representation of the class
\mathfrak{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
of odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains by the class
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{G}}
of bunches of layer groups, published in
[
10
]
, to a categorical equivalence between the category
\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
of odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains equipped with their normal homomorphisms and the category
\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}
of bunches of layer groups equipped with bunch homomorphisms, defined here.
A consequence of our results is that a rich segment of weakening-free substructural logics can be investigated by using age-old knowledge and techniques in the field of abelian
o
-groups, see
[
9
]
for example.

FL
e
-algebras are algebraic semantics for substructural logics with the exchange property
[
5
]
. In (substructural) fuzzy logics
[
12
]
the chains of the related variety play a fundamental role, this makes the algebraic investigation of FL
e
-chains a cutting edge of the research in fuzzy logics.
An
FL
e
-algebra
1
1

1

Other terminologies for FL
e
-algebras are: pointed commutative residuated lattices or pointed commutative residuated lattice-ordered monoids.

is a structure
\mathbf{X}=(X,\leq,\cdot,\mathbin{\rightarrow},t,f)
such that

(X,\leq)
is a lattice,
(X,\leq,\cdot,t)
is a commutative monoid,
f
is an arbitrary constant
2
2

2

Called the
falsum
constant.
,
and

{x}{y}\leq z

3
3

3

If the multiplication operation is clear from the context, we write
{x}{y}
for
{x}\cdot{y}
, as usual.
iff
{x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{z}\geq y
.
The latter condition, called residuation condition, is equivalent to the following: for any
x,z
, the set
\{v\ |\ {x}{v}\leq z\}
has its greatest element, and
{x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{z}
, the residuum of
x
and
z
, is defined as this element:
{x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{z}:=\max\{v\ |\ {x}{v}\leq z\}
.
Commutative residuated lattices
are the
f
-free reducts of FL
e
-algebras.
Being residuated implies that
\cdot
is lattice ordered, that is
\cdot
distributes over join.
One defines the
residual complement operation
by
{x}{{}^{\prime}}={x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{f}
and calls an FL
e
-algebra
involutive
if
{({x}{{}^{\prime}})}{{}^{\prime}}=x
holds.
In the involutive case
{x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{y}={({x}{{y}{{}^{\prime}}})}{{}^{\prime}}
holds.
Call an element
x\geq t

positive
.
An involutive FL
e
-algebra is called
odd
if the residual complement operation leaves the unit element fixed, that is,
{t}{{}^{\prime}}=t
, and
even
if the following (two) quasi-identities hold:
x<t

\Leftrightarrow

x\leq f
.
The former condition is equivalent to
f=t
, while
the latter quasi-identities are equivalent to assuming that

f

is the lower cover of

t
(and
t

is the upper cover of

f
)
if chains are considered, that is, when the order is total.

An original decomposition method along with the related construction method has been introduced in
[
10
]
for the class of odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains.
The main idea was to partition the algebra with the help of its local unit function
x\mapsto{x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}}{x}

into a direct system of (hopefully simpler, “nicer”) algebras, indexed by the positive idempotent elements of the original algebra, with transitions of the direct system defined by multiplication with a positive idempotent element,
and to rebuild the algebra from the direct system using a construction which partly coincides with the construction of Płonka sums.
It is called layer algebra decomposition.
This idea has successfully been applied recently for other classes of residuated lattices including
finite commutative idempotent involutive residuated lattices in
[
11
]
,
locally integral involutive po-monoids and semirings in
[
8
]
,
and very recently for Bochvar algebras in
[
1
]
.
In these classes the layer algebras are “nice”.
In
[
10
]
, however, the layer algebras are only somewhat nicer than the original algebra, therefore a second step, a layer algebras to layer groups construction (and reconstruction) phase had to be included.
The layer algebra decomposition together with this second phase shall be presented as a single step in Theorem
2.5
below.
The main result of the present paper is to lift the correspondence between odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains and bunches of layer groups, described in Theorem
2.5
, to a categorical equivalence between the category

\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}

of odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains with FL
e
-algebra homomorphisms
and
the category

\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}

of bunches of layer groups with bunch homomorphisms.
Restricting the related functor to subcategories yields further categorical equivalences.

2.
A bijective correspondence between
\mathfrak{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{G}}

For a partially ordered set
\mathbf{X}=(X,\leq)
and for
x\in X
define the upper neighbor
x_{\uparrow}
of
x
to be the unique cover of
x
if such exists, and
x
otherwise.
Define
x_{\downarrow}
dually.
A partially ordered algebra with a poset reduct will be called
discretely ordered
if for any element
x
,
x_{\downarrow}<x<x_{\uparrow}
holds.
A directed partially ordered set is a partially ordered set such that every pair of elements has an upper bound.

Definition 2.1
.

Let
\langle\kappa,\leq\rangle
be a directed partially ordered set.
Let
\{\mathbf{A}_{i}\in\mathfrak{U}:i\in\kappa\}
be a family of algebras of the same type and
\varsigma_{i\to j}
be a homomorphism
4
4

4

Homomorphisms are understood according to the corresponding setting. We shall call them the transitions of the direct system.
for every
i,j\in\kappa
,
i\leq j
with the following properties:

(1)

\varsigma_{i\to i}
is the identity of
\mathbf{A}_{i}
, and

(2)

\varsigma_{i\to k}=\varsigma_{j\to k}\circ\varsigma_{i\to j}
for all
i\leq j\leq k
.

Then
\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\varsigma_{i\to j}\rangle
is called a direct system of algebras in
\mathfrak{U}
over
\kappa
.

Definition 2.2
.

Let
\pazocal A=\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},f_{i\to j}\rangle_{\langle\alpha,\leq_{\alpha}\rangle}

\Phi:\pazocal A\to\pazocal B

a system of
\mathfrak{U}

for every

the diagram in Fig.

We shall need the following lemma.

Lemma 2.4
.

For any bunch of layer groups

\langle\textbf{{G${}_{u}$}},\textbf{{H${}_{u}$}},\varsigma_{u\to v}\rangle_{\langle\kappa_{o},\kappa_{J},\kappa_{I},\leq_{\kappa}\rangle}

with

\textbf{{G${}_{u}$}}=(G_{u},\preceq_{u},\cdot_{u},\ {}^{-1_{u}},u)
, if
u\in\kappa_{J}
then

(2.3)

x\cdot_{u}u_{\downarrow_{u}}=x_{\downarrow_{u}},

Proof.

Since
u\in\kappa_{J}
,
the lower cover
x_{\downarrow_{u}}
of
x
exists
in
G_{u}

for any
x\in G_{u}
,
see
(G3)
.
Since
\cdot_{u}

is cancellative,

x\cdot_{u}u_{\downarrow_{u}}\prec_{u}x\cdot_{u}u=x

holds on the one hand, while on the other,

x\cdot_{u}u_{\downarrow_{u}}\prec_{u}y\prec_{u}x

for some
y\in G_{u}

would lead, by multiplying with
x^{-1_{u}}
, to an element in
G_{u}
which is strictly in between
u_{\downarrow_{u}}
and
u
, a contradiction.
∎

Remark 2.6
.

If Definition
2.3
is slightly modified in such a way that the

\accentset{\bullet}{\textbf{{H${}_{u}$}}}
’s
are “stored” in the definition of a bunch
(like
{\pazocal G}=\langle\textbf{{G${}_{u}$}},\textbf{{H${}_{u}$}},\accentset{\bullet}{\textbf{{H${}_{u}$}}},\varsigma_{u\to v}\rangle_{\langle\kappa_{o},\kappa_{J},\kappa_{I},\leq_{\kappa}\rangle}
),
and
instead of taking a copy
\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{u}
of
H_{u}
in (
2.13
), that stored copy is used in the construction of Theorem
2.5
/
(A)
,
then also

\mathbf{X}_{(\pazocal G_{\mathbf{X}})}=\mathbf{X}

holds.
Then, Theorem
2.5
describes a bijection, in a constructive manner, between the classes
\mathfrak{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{G}}
.
Stating only the isomorphism will be sufficient for our purposes in the present paper.
See also Remark
3.7
.

3.
Categorical equivalence between
\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
and
\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}

We start with a few lemmata to ease the subsequent computation.

Lemma 3.1
.

For
x\in X
,
(
2.22
) can equivalently be written as

(3.1)

{x}{{}^{\prime}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\left(\gamma_{u}(x)^{-1_{u}}\right)^{\bullet}&\mbox{ if $u\in\kappa_{I}$ and $x\in H_{u}$}\\
{\gamma_{u}(x)^{-1_{u}}}_{\downarrow_{u}}&\mbox{ if $u\in\kappa_{J}$ and $x\in G_{u}$}\\
\gamma_{u}(x)^{-1_{u}}&\mbox{ otherwise}\\
\end{array}\right..

For
x,y\in X
,
(
2.20
) can equivalently be written as

(3.2)

{x}\mathbin{{\mathbin{*\mkern-9.0mu\circ}}_{u}}{y}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\left({\gamma_{u}(x)}\cdot_{u}{\gamma_{u}(y)}\right)^{\bullet}&\mbox{ if
$u\in\kappa_{I}$,
${\gamma_{u}(x)}\cdot_{u}{\gamma_{u}(y)}\in H_{u}$,
$\neg(x,y\in H_{u})$
}\\
{\gamma_{u}(x)}\cdot_{u}{\gamma_{u}(y)}&\mbox{ otherwise}\\
\end{array}\right..

Proof.

C.f. (
2.16
).
∎

Lemma 3.2
.

For
u\in\kappa_{I}
, the definition of the ordering in (
2.18
) can equivalently be given by any of the following ones.

(3.3)

\begin{array}[]{ll}\mbox{
$x\leq_{u}y$
iff
$\gamma_{u}(x)\prec_{u}\gamma_{u}(y)$
or
$\left(\mbox{
$\gamma_{u}(x)=\gamma_{u}(y)$
and
$(x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{u}$ or $y\in G_{u})$
}\right)$
}\end{array}

(3.4)

\begin{array}[]{ll}\mbox{
$x<_{u}y$
iff
$\gamma_{u}(x)\prec_{u}\gamma_{u}(y)$
or
$\left(\mbox{
$\gamma_{u}(x)=\gamma_{u}(y)$,
$x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{u}$,
$y\in G_{u}$
}\right)$
}\end{array}

Proof.

For
u\in\kappa_{I}
, the meaning of the definition of the ordering in (
2.18
) is that for any element
a
in a subgroup, its dotted copy
\accentset{\bullet}{a}
is inserted just below
a
.
Any of (
3.3
) and (
3.4
) expresses the same.
∎

Throughout the paper we adopt the notation of Definition
2.3
and Theorem
2.5
, with appropriate superscripts
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
or
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
if more than one algebra is involved in the discussion.

Definition 3.3
.

Let

\begin{array}[]{l}{\pazocal X}=\langle\textbf{{G${}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$}},\textbf{{H${}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$}},\varsigma_{u\to with $\textbf{{G${}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$}}=(G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u},\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u},\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u},\ {}^{-1^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}},u),$}\\
{\pazocal with $\textbf{{G${}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{u}$}}=(G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{u},\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{u},\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{u},\ be bunches of layer groups.
Let

(3.5)

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}=\displaystyle\dot{\bigcup}_{u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

and

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}=\displaystyle\dot{\bigcup}_{s\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{s}
.

We say that

(3.6)

\Phi:G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}\to G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

is a
bunch homomorphism
from
\pazocal X
to
\pazocal Y

if

(S1)

\Phi|_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}
maps into
\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
and is isotone: if
u,v\in{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}
,

u\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
then
\Phi(u)\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)
,

(S2)

for
u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
,

(3.7)

\Phi_{u}:=\Phi|_{G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}}

is an
o
-group homomorphism from

G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
into

G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}
,

(S3)

\Phi
commutes with the transitions:

for
u,v\in{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}
,
u\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
,
the following diagram commutes.

{diagram}

(S4)

Proof.

We adopt the notation of Theorem
2.5
with the appropriate superscript
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
or
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
.
A mapping
\varphi
is a homomorphism
from
\mathbf{X}
to
\mathbf{Y}

if and only if the following conditions hold.
For
x,y\in X
,

(1)

\varphi(X)\subseteq Y
,

(2)

x\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}y
implies

\varphi(x)\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\varphi(y)
,

(3)

\varphi({x}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{y})={\varphi(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\varphi(y)}

(S1)
:
For
u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
,

u
is a positive idempotent element in
X
by (
2.5
),
hence
\varphi(u)
is a positive idempotent element in
Y
(see
5
and
2
for positivity and
3
for idempotence), and thus
\Phi(u)\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
by (
2.5
).
Let
u,v\in{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}
,

u\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
.
Then
u\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}v
by (
2.5
).
It holds true that,
u\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_IgyNeznekKi})}{\in}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

, hence

.

,

and
since

,

\Phi(u)\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)

by (

).

(S2)
:
Let
u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{458392}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{458392}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{575987}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
.
Until the end of the proof of
(S7)
, we denote, for short,

(3.12)

w:=\varphi(u)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\Phi(u)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\Phi_{u}(u).

First we prove that
w
is the unit element of
G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
.
By (
2.5
),
u
is a positive idempotent element in
X
,
and
u
is the unit element of
G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
, c.f. (
3.8
).
Since positive idempotent elements are mapped into positive idempotent elements by homomorphisms, and since for every layer group there corresponds a single positive idempotent element which is its unit element and which indexes that particular layer group, it follows that
\Phi_{u}
, which coincides with
\varphi
over
G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

by (
3.11
), preserves the unit element.

Next we prove that
\Phi_{u}
maps
G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
into
G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
.
Let
x\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
.
Then

{\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(x)}\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}{\varphi(x)}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\varphi(x)}\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_ReSTrictITE}}{=}\varphi({x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{x})=\varphi(u)\overset{(\ref{EzaV})}{=}w
, hence
\Phi_{u}(x)\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_XHiGYkESZUL})}{\in}L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
.
If
w\notin\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
then

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_DEFcsopi})}{=}L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
and we are done.
Assume
w\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
.
Then

u\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_particio}}{\in}\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

and
we need to prove that
\Phi_{u}(x)
is not in
{\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}
, c.f. (
2.8
).
Contrary, assume

\Phi_{u}(x)\in{\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}.

By (
2.4
),

H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}\subseteq G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}

and by
(
2.8
),

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}\cap\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}=\emptyset
, hence

H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}\cap\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}=\emptyset
follows, and thus

\Phi_{u}(x)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\varphi(x)
is not
w
-invertible (with respect to
\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}
) by (
2.7
).
Since
\varphi
maps
u
-invertible elements into
w
-invertible ones by
3
and (
3.12
),

x
cannot be
u
-invertible, hence
x\notin H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
by (
2.7
).
Therefore,

x\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}\setminus H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
.
Since
u\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
,
by the first two rows of (
2.22
),

{}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}
maps
H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
into
\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
and vice versa,
hence

{x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}\setminus H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

and

{H_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\overset{\Phi_{u}(x)\in{\accentset{\bullet}{H}_{w}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}{\ni}{\Phi_{u}(x)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}{\varphi(x)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_ReSTrictITE},\re which,
since

is the unit element of

{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
,
leads to

,
