w\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
then

{({({\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(y)})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{w})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{w}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_IgyTorzulaSzorzat})}{=}{\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}}{\Phi_{u}(y)}
,
whereas if
w\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
then
w=t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}=f^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
holds by Table
1
,
and hence

{({({\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(y)})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{w})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{w}={({({\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(y)})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{f}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}})}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{f}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}={{({\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(y)})}{{}^{\prime}}}{{}^{\prime}}={\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(y)}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_IgyNeznekKi})}{=}{\Phi_{u}(x)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}}{\Phi_{u}(y)}
.

Summing up, we have shown that
\Phi_{u}
is a group homomorphism from

G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

to

G
{}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
.
Finally, we verify that
\Phi_{u}
is an
o
-group homomorphism.
Let
x,y\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
,
x\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}y
.
By (
2.15
)–(
2.18
),

x\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}y
and thus by
2
,

\Phi_{u}(x)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\varphi(x)\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}\varphi(y)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\Phi_{u}(y)
follows.
Since we have already shown that
\Phi_{u}(x),\Phi_{u}(y)\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
, by (
2.15
)–(
2.18
)
we obtain

\Phi_{u}(x)\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}\Phi_{u}(y)
.

(S3)
:
For
x\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
,

\Phi_{v}(\varsigma_{u\to v}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x))\overset{(\ref{tipusSIGMA}),\,(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\varphi(\varsigma_{u\to v}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x))\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_MapAzSzorzas})}{=}\varphi({v}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{x})\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_ReSTrict}}{=}{\varphi(v)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\varphi(x)}\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}{\Phi(v)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{\Phi_{u}(x)}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_MapAzSzorzas}),\,\ref{KATEGOR_embedding}}{=}\varsigma_{\Phi(u)\to\Phi(v)}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}(\Phi_{u}(x))
.

(S4)
:
By (
2.5
),
t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
is the least element of

\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
, and
t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
is the least element of

\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
. Thus the claim follows from
5
.

(S6)
:
Assume
u\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

and

w\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
.
If
x\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
then by (
2.7
),

x
is
u
-invertible (with respect to
\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}
), and hence
\Phi_{u}(x)
must be
w
-invertible (with respect to
\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}
, by
(S2)
and (
3.12
)) and thus
\Phi_{u}(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
by (
2.7
), as stated.
Assume

x\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}\setminus H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
.
Then

{x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
holds
by
(
2.22
).
Therefore,

\Phi_{u}(x)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\varphi(x)=\varphi\left({\left({x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)={\varphi\left({x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}{\Phi_{u}\left({x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}
.
Hence if, contrary to the statement,

\Phi_{u}(x)

were in

H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}

then

{\Phi_{u}\left({x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}

were in

H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
, too,
and
using that

{}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}
maps
H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
to
\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}

(see the second row of (
2.22
)),

\Phi_{u}\left({x}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)
would be in

\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}
,
a contradiction to
(S2)
.

(S7)
:

Suppose that
u\in\kappa_{J}
.
It holds true that

\Phi\left({u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)\overset{(\ref{EQmindenPHI})}{=}\varphi\left({u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_ReSTrictITE},\ref{KATEGOR_FkegyenlOEk}}{=}{\varphi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{EzaV})}{=}{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}
.
Therefore,

\Phi(x_{\downarrow_{u}})\overset{(\ref{PhiSlice})}{=}\Phi_{u}(x_{\downarrow_{u}})\overset{(\ref{87JHxdhFldD})}{=}\Phi_{u}\left(x\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}u_{\downarrow_{u}}\right)\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_SplitNega})}{=}\Phi_{u}\left(x\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}{u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_homomorfizmus}}{=}\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{v}\Phi_{u}\left({u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\right)=\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}.

(1)

It holds true that

\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_SplitNega})}{=}\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}w_{\downarrow_{w}}\overset{(\ref{87JHxdhFldD})}{=}\Phi_{u}(x)_{\downarrow_{w}}

if
w\in{\kappa_{J}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}
,
and hence

\Phi(x_{\downarrow_{u}})=\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}={\Phi_{u}(x)}_{\downarrow_{w}}\overset{(\ref{PhiSlice})}{=}{\Phi(x)}_{\downarrow_{w}}
.

(2)

If

w\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
then

\Phi(\mathbf{X})
is odd and

is its unit element, see Table
1
, and hence

{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}=w
holds.
Therefore,

\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}{w}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}=\Phi_{u}(x)\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{w}w=\Phi_{u}(x)\overset{(\ref{PhiSlice})}{=}\Phi(x)
.

(S8)
:
By adopting the conditions of
(S8)
,
by
(

),

\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(y)\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}

implies

\varsigma_{u\to $\accentset{\bullet}{a}=x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$ and $\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}$},\\
\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{u}(a)}\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_IkszU}),(\ref{KATEGOR_EZazX})}{\subseteq}Y&\mbox{ if $\accentset{\bullet}{a}=x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$ and $\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}$}.\\
\end{array}\right.

2
:
Let
x,y\in X
.
Then
x\in L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
and

for some

, c.f. (
2.12
).
Assume
x<^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}y
.
By (
2.15
),
it
is equivalent to

\mbox{

$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(x)=\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(y)$ and $u<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v$
},

We need to prove

\varphi(x)\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\varphi(y)

is equivalent to

or
$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(x))=\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(y))$ and
$\Phi(u)\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)$
},

\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{v\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y)))&\mbox{ otherwise}\\
\end{array}\right.\end{array}.\end{array}

We shall prove only the first one, the other proof is analogous.
For

x\in L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
,

\color[rgb]{150,173,180}\small\begin{array}[]{ccl}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(x))&\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_P5})}{=}&\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)\to\Phi(v)}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(x)))&\mbox{ &\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_ViSSZaEpul})}{=}&\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)\to\Phi(v)}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\color[rgb]{150,173,180})&\mbox{ if $\Phi(u)<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431807}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)$}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))}&\mbox{ \color[rgb]{0,0,0} if $x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$, \color[rgb]{0,0,0}$\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\ni\color[rgb]{150,173,180}\Phi(u)\geq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431807}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)$}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))&\mbox{ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0} otherwise}}\\

&\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_commutes},\,(\ref{KATEGOR_ViSSZaEpulCALC})}{=}&\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Phi_{v}(\varsigma_{u\to v}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\color[rgb]{150,173,180}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)\color[rgb]{0,0,0})))&\mbox{ \color[rgb]{150,173,180}if $\Phi(u)<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431807}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)$}\\
\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))}&\mbox{ \color[rgb]{150,173,180}
if $x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$, $\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\ni\Phi(u)\geq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431807}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)$}\\
\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696132}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575743}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))&\mbox{ \color[rgb]{150,173,180}
otherwise}\\
\end{array}\right.\end{array}.

In the first row,
u<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
follows from

\Phi(u)<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)

by
(S1)
, hence
v=uv
and thus we may write the value in that row as

\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))
.
Consider the last two rows.
If
u\geq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
then
u=uv

and thus
by
1

we may write the values as

\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))}

and

\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))
,
respectively.
Finally, if
u<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
then

\Phi(u)\leq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)
holds
by
(S1)
, hence we obtain

\Phi(u)=\Phi(v)
.
Therefore,
\Phi_{u}=\Phi_{v}\circ\varsigma_{u\to v}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

holds by
(S3)
,
and thus also here
we can write the values as

\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))}

and

\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))
,
respectively.
Summing up, (
3.19
) holds.
Consequently,

(3.20)

\begin{array}[]{r}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(x)))=\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))),\\

(b)

If

y\in\accentset{\bullet}{H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}}

for

and

\Phi(v)\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
)
then

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}\overset{(\ref{tipusSIGMA})}{\ni}\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u\to v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\leq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}y\overset{(\ref{DEFgamma})}{\leq}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y)\in
