implies

\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u\to v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_KibovitettRendezesITTIS})}{\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y)

and hence

\Phi_{v}(\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u\to v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_homomorfizmus}}{\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(v)}}\Phi_{v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y))
,
which together with

\varphi(y)\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_ViSSZaEpulCALC})}{\in}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(v)}

and in turn,

\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(v)}(\varphi(y))\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_P5})}{\in}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(v)}

ensures (
3.21
).

Assume

u=v
.
By (
3.17
),
(
3.16
)
is equivalent to

x<^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}y
,
which, by (
3.4
)
is equivalent to

(3.22)

\mbox{
$\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)\prec_{u}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(y)$
or
$(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)=\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(y)$,
$u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{I}$,
$x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}$,
$y\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u})$
\par}.

(a)

If
y\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}

then

\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(y))\overset{(\ref{ezLETT})}{=}\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(y))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_homomorfizmus}}{\in}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}

holds, and hence
(
3.21
) is equivalent to

\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(y))

which follows from (

) by
(S2)
.

(b)

Therefore, it remains to assume
y\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
for

.
Therefore,
if
\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
then

\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_injective}}{\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}}\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(y))

holds and hence so does (

),
whereas

then

\Phi_{v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_homomorfizmus}}{\preceq^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(v)}}\Phi_{v}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y))

together with

- If

- If

\accentset{\bullet}{\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v\to uv}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}(y)))}
, and since by (

)

)

{\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u\to (ii)

Assume

.

\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

since

\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\ni\Phi(u)\overset{Table~\ref{KATEGOR_ThetaPsiOmegaAGAIN}}{=}t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}>_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}\Phi(v)

is impossible.

,

) is equivalent to

(3.25)

\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(x))<^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(y)).

3
:
Let
x,y\in X
.
Then
x\in L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
and
y\in L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{v}
for some
u,v\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
, c.f. (
2.12
).
First we prove that if
\Phi(uv)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

then

(3.26)

\begin{array}[]{l}\mbox{
$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}$
if and only if
$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(x))\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}$.
}\\
\mbox{
$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(y)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}$
if and only if
$\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(y))\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}$.
}\end{array}

Indeed,
by (
3.15
),
if
\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

then

\varphi(x)\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}

is equivalent to

x\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
.
Therefore, by
(S6)
,
(S5)
, and the first row of (
3.15
),

\varphi(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}
is equivalent to

x\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
.
Therefore, if

u\geq_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v
then

\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}(\varphi(x))\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_P5})}{=}\varphi(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}

is equivalent to

H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}\ni x\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_P5})}{=}\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(x)
,
whereas if
u<_{\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}v

then
both
\rho^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}(x)\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_P5})}{=}\varsigma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u\to uv}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_G2}}{\in}H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}

&\overset{(\ref{EziStrUe})}{=}&\left\{\color[rgb]{150,173,180}\begin{array}[]{ll}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(x)))^{-1^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}}\right)^{\bullet}&\mbox{ if $\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}$,
$\varphi(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(x))^{-1^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}}\right)_{\downarrow_{\Phi(u)}}\par&\mbox{ if $\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{J}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}$,
$\varphi(x)\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}$
}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}(\varphi(x))^{-1^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}}&\mbox{ otherwise}\\
\end{array}\right.\\
&\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_SplitNegaSIMP})}{=}&{\varphi(x)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}},\end{array}

hence

\varphi\left({x}\mathbin{\mathbin{\rightarrow}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{y}\right)\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_IgYaReSi})}{=}\varphi\left({\left({x}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{{y}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}\ri .

5
:

\varphi(t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}})\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_ViSSZaEpul})}{=}\Phi_{t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{696131}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{575740}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}(t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}})\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_tLESZez}),\,\ref{KATEGOR_legkisebbelem}}{=}t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

.

.

(3)
Since by (
3.9
),
\Phi
arises from
\varphi
by restriction, it suffices to show that any bunch homomorphism
\Phi

uniquely
extends to a homomorphism
\varphi
. To this end, assume that
\varphi
is a homomorphism,
and that its restriction to
G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
is the bunch homomorphism
\Phi
.
We shall prove that
\varphi
coincides with the one given in (
3.13
).
Since the first row of (
3.13
) obviously holds,
let
x\in\accentset{\bullet}{H}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}
for some
u\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}
.
It holds true by (
2.7
) that

x={y}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{{u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}}
for some

y\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_G2},\,(\ref{PHIdomain})}{\subseteq}G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

and

y\overset{(\ref{DEFgamma})}{=}\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{u}(x)
.
Therefore,

\varphi(x)=\varphi({y}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{{u}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}}}}}})={\varphi(y)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{{\varphi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}}
,
where

\varphi(y)\overset{y\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{=}\Phi(y)\overset{(\ref{PhiSlice})}{=}\Phi_{u}(y)\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_subgroups_and_complements}}{\in}H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}
.
By
(S5)
,
\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}\cup\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
.
If

\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{o}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

then

{\varphi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{u\in G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{522098}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.99998pt}{\resizebox{431808}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\resizebox{431805}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}}{=}{\Phi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{PhiSlice})}{=}{\Phi_{u}(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{Table~\ref{KATEGOR_ThetaPsiOmegaAGAIN}}{=}{t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.1666 is the unit element for
L^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(u)}
, and hence

{\varphi(y)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{{\varphi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}}={\Phi_{u}(y)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{t^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}=\Phi_{u}(y)=\Phi_{u}(\gamma^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\r ,
whereas if

\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}

then

{\varphi(y)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{{\varphi(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}}={\Phi_{u}(y)}\mathbin{\cdot^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}{{\Phi_{u}(u)}^{{\prime^{\mkern-4.0mu{}^{{}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{609115}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16664pt}{\resizebox{503776}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.16666pt}{\resizebox{503772}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}}}}}}}\overset{(\ref{KATEGOR_XHiGYkESZUL})}{=}\accentset{\bullet}{\Ph .
∎

Proposition 3.5
.

The category
\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}
is well-defined.

Proof.

We shall verify that
the function composition of two bunch homomorphisms
is a bunch homomorphism, that this composition is associative,
and that the identity mapping is a bunch homomorphism and also an identity morphism.
Adapt the notation of Definition
3.3
for three bunches of layer groups
\pazocal X
,
\pazocal Y
, and
\pazocal Z
, and
let
\Phi
and
\Psi
be bunch homomorphisms from
\pazocal X
to
\pazocal Y
and from
\pazocal Y
to
\pazocal Z
, respectively.
Obviously,
\Psi\circ\Phi
is of type

G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}\to G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
, and it is also obvious that
\Psi\circ\Phi
satisfies properties
(S1)
–
(S5)
.
As for
(S6)
, if

u\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

and

(\Psi\circ\Phi)(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{550508}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{550508}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{700022}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{517490}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}}

then from the latter, by
(S5)
,

\Phi(u)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
follows, and hence

\Psi\circ\Phi

clearly satisfies

(S6)
, too.
Invoking
(S5)
in the very same manner as above shows that

\Psi\circ\Phi
satisfies
(S7)a
.
To prove
(S7)b
, notice that since
u\in\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{J}
, it follows from
(S5)
that
\Phi(u)
is either in
\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{J}
or in
\kappa^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{o}
. In either case application of
(S7)
for
\Phi
and then for
\Psi
to concludes the proof of
(S7)b
.
As for
(S8)
, from

(\Psi\circ\Phi)(uv)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{550508}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{550508}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{700022}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{517490}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Z})$}}}}}}

it follows by
(S5)
that
\Phi(uv)\in\kappa_{I}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}
,
hence

\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(y)\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x)\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}_{uv}

implies, by using
(S8)
for
\Phi
,
that

\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{v\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(y))\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}\Phi_{uv}(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x))\overset{\ref{KATEGOR_subgroups_and_complements}}{\in}H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}
,
that is,

\varsigma_{\Phi(v)\to\Phi(uv)}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}(\Phi_{u}(y))\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}\varsigma_{\Phi(u)\to\Phi(uv)}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}(\Phi_{u}(x))\in H^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{\Phi(uv)}

by
(S3)
,
and hence by
(S8)
for
\Psi
,

(\Psi_{\Phi(uv)}\circ\Phi_{uv})(\varsigma_{v\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(y))\prec^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{Y})$}}}}}}_{(\Psi\circ\Phi)(uv)}(\Psi_{\Phi(uv)}\circ\Phi_{uv})(\varsigma_{u\to uv}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}(x))
.

It is obvious to verify that the identity mapping on
G^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\displaystyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33333pt}{\resizebox{611189}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.09999pt}{$\textstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{767983}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.46999pt}{$\scriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.33331pt}{\resizebox{575744}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.04999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(\mathbf{X})$}}}}}}

satisfies
(S1)
–
(S8)
, so it is a bunch homomorphism.
Composition of functions is associative, hence so is the composition of bunch homomorphisms, cf. (
3.6
), which also makes it immediate that the identity mapping is an identity morphism.
∎

Theorem 3.6
.

The functor
\Upsilon:\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}\to\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}

from the category of odd or even involutive FL
e
-chains with FL
e
-algebra homomorphisms
to the category of bunches of layer groups with bunch homomorphisms
given by

\Upsilon\mathbf{X}=\pazocal G_{\mathbf{X}}

(see Theorem
2.5
/
(A)
)
and

\Upsilon\varphi=\Phi

(see (
3.9
))
defines a categorical equivalence,
its inverse functor
\Upsilon^{-1}
being

\Upsilon^{-1}\pazocal G=\mathbf{X}_{\pazocal G}

(see Theorem
2.5
/
(B)
)
and

\Upsilon^{-1}\Phi=\varphi

(see (
3.10
)).
∎

Proof.

A functor
F:C\to D
is known to yield an equivalence of categories if and only if it is simultaneously fully faithful, i.e., for any two objects
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
of
C
, the map
Hom_{C}(c_{1},c_{2})\to Hom_{D}(Fc_{1},Fc_{2})
induced by
F
is bijective; and
essentially surjective (dense), i.e. each object
d
in
D
is isomorphic to an object of the form
Fc
, for
c
in
C
.

\Upsilon
satisfies these by
Theorem
2.5
and Lemma
3.4
.
∎

Remark 3.7
.

In fact, the functor
\Upsilon
exhibits a categorical isomorphism between
\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}
and
\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}
if in the definition of the latter the objects are modified in accordance with Remark
2.6
.

Remark 3.8
.

By Theorem
2.5
/
(B)
, odd (resp. even)
involutive FL
e
-chains correspond bunches of layer groups where
t\in\kappa_{0}
(resp.
t\notin\kappa_{0}
).
By
[
10
, Example 8.2]
,
even Sugihara chains correspond to bunches of layer groups of the form

\langle\mathbbm{1}_{u},\mathbbm{1}_{u},\varsigma_{u\to v}\rangle_{\langle\emptyset,\emptyset,\kappa,\leq_{\kappa}\rangle}
,
and
odd Sugihara chains correspond bunches of layer groups of the form

\langle\mathbbm{1}_{u},\mathbbm{1}_{u},\varsigma_{u\to v}\rangle_{\langle\{t\},\emptyset,\kappa\setminus\{t\},\leq_{\kappa}\rangle}
,
where
\mathbbm{1}
denotes the trivial (one-element) group.
By restricting the objects of

\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}

and

\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}

to these classes, respectively,
as a corollary of Theorem
3.6

we obtain categorical equivalences between the above described subcategories of

\mathscr{I}^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\mathfrak{0}\mathfrak{1}}

and

\mathscr{B}_{\mathscr{G}}
, respectively.
